# Wiktionary Maltese - Letter T (Refined)

## benġila (n-benġila)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bruise
    **MT**: Marka skura fuq il-ġilda kkawżata minn daqqa jew laqta.
- **Alternative Forms**: tbenġila
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Wara l-waqgħa kellu benġila kbira fuq rirsu.
    EN: After the fall he had a large bruise on his leg.

## intavla (v-intavla)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to plank, board up
    **MT**: Għatta jew saqqa b'bosta twaħħal u twaqqiegħ tal-injam.
- **Alternative Forms**: ttavla
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Intavla l-bieb tal-maħżen biex ma jidħol ħadd.
    EN: He boarded up the store door so no one would enter.

---

## intrakka (v-intrakka)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to dock, moor (a ship)
    **MT**: Rabatha u pparja vapur jew dgħajsa fil-moll u port.
- **Alternative Forms**: trakka
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-vapur intrakka fil-port nazzjonali wara s-safra.
    EN: The ship docked at the national port after the voyage.

## ittewweb (v-ittewweb)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to yawn
    **MT**: Fetaħ ħalqu u ħa nifs twil minħabba għeja jew binja ta' rrad.
- **Alternative Forms**: ttewweb
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ittewweb bosta drabi waqt il-laqgħa twila.
    EN: He yawned several times during the long meeting.

## ittiekel (v-ittiekel)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:

- **Alternative Forms**: ttiekel
- **Usage Examples**:

    EN: All the food was eaten by the villagers and elders.

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ittraduċa l-ktieb mill-Malti għall-Ingliż bil-ħefda.

- **POS**: interjection
- **Root**: għ-t-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: mind you, look (postpositioned particle)

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Hekk għandha ssir ix-ħidma, ta!
    EN: That is how the work should be done, mind you!

## ta (v-ta)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-t-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to give
    **MT**: Għadda jew ta oġġett lil xi ħadd minn id għal id.

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ta rigal sabiħ lil ommu f'għeluq sninha.
    EN: He gave a beautiful gift to his mother on her birthday.

- **POS**: verb

## tliegħab (v-tliegħab)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to play around, trifle
    **MT**: Mar u qagħad jilgħab bla impenn dwar ix-xogħol.
- **Alternative Forms**: tliegħeb
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Tliegħab mar-regoli u tilef l-unur.
    EN: He played around with the rules and lost honor.

---

## trajjef (n-trajjef)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: t-r-f
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: little edge (diminutive)
    **MT**: Xifer jew tarf żgħir ta' oġġett jew raba'.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Rranġa it-trajjef tal-karta bir-riga.
    EN: He adjusted the small edge of the paper with a ruler.

---

## trajjes (n-trajjes)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: t-r-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: little shield (diminutive)
    **MT**: Tarka żgħira użata mil-gwerrier f'difiża.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Zamm it-trajjes b'id waħda waqt il-battalja.
    EN: He held the small shield with one hand during battle.

---

## trakk (n-trakk)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: truck, lorry
    **MT**: Vettura kbira u b'saħħitha użata biex iġorr merkanzija heavy.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-trakk ġarr il-ġebel lejn il-post tal-bini.
    EN: The truck carried the stones to the building site.

---

## trakka (v-trakka)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to dock, berth (ship)
    **MT**: Daħal u rabat il-bastiment mal-moll tal-port.
- **Alternative Forms**: ttrakka, intrakka
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-vapur trakka fil-port ta' Marsamxett filgħaxija.
    EN: The ship docked in Marsamxett harbor in the evening.

---

## tramuntana (n-tramuntana)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: north
    **MT**: Naħa jew direzzjoni tas-sema opposta għan-nofsinhar.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ir-riħ mit-tramuntana ġab miegħu kesħa kbira.
    EN: The wind from the north brought great cold with it.

---

## tranżizzjoni (n-tranżizzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: transition
    **MT**: Pass u bidla minn stat jew pjan għal ieħor.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tranżizzjoni lejn it-teknoloġija l-ġdida marret sew.
    EN: The transition to the new technology went well.

---

## traqqa' (v-traqqa)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: r-q-għ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to be patched, mended
    **MT**: Ġie msejjes u rranġat b'biċċa oħra ta' drapp.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-qmis traqqat min-nanna bil-labra u l-ħajta.
    EN: The shirt was patched by grandmother with needle and thread.

---

## trattat (n-trattat)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: treaty, pact
    **MT**: Ftehim u dokument uffiċjali bejn żewġ pajjiżi jew gvernijiet.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-ambaxxaturi ffirmaw it-trattat ta' paċi fil-belt.
    EN: The ambassadors signed the peace treaty in the city.

---

## trattativa (n-trattativa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: negotiation, bargaining
    **MT**: Diskussjoni u tiftix ta' ftehim dwar prezz jew liġi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-trattativa dwar il-pagi bdiet kmieni fl-uffiċċju.
    EN: The negotiation regarding wages started early in the office.

---

## trawwem (v-trawwem)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: r-w-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to acquire proficiency, be trained
    **MT**: Kiber u tgħallem u rċieva taħriġ sod f'ħidma.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Trawwem fis-sengħa mill-mastro tiegħu sa mill-tfulija.
    EN: He was trained in the craft by his master since childhood.

---

## trawwim (n-trawwim)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: r-w-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: training, acquiring proficiency
    **MT**: Proċess ta' tagħlim u bini ta' kapaċitajiet f'wieħed.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-trawwim ta' ħaddiema ġodda jsaħħaħ l-azjenda.
    EN: Training new workers strengthens the company.

---

## traċċa (n-traċċa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: trace, footprint, mark
    **MT**: Marka jew sinjal imħallef minn persuna jew annimal.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Sab traċċa ta' footprints fit-tajn tar-raba'.
    EN: He found a trace of footprints in the field mud.

---

## traġedja (n-traġedja)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: tragedy
    **MT**: Ġrajja u episodju ta' niket kbir u telf ta' ħajja.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-traġedja fil-baħar ġabet niket nazzjonali kbir.
    EN: The tragedy at sea brought great national grief.

---

## traġikament (adv-traġikament)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: tragically
    **MT**: B'mod li jġib niket kbir u diżastru.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Traġikament tilef ħajtu fl-inċident tat-triq.
    EN: Tragically he lost his life in the road accident.

---

## traġiku (adj-traġiku)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: tragic
    **MT**: Li għandu karattru u riżultat ta' niket u diżastru.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm riżultat traġiku meta l-bini waqa'.
    EN: There was a tragic result when the building collapsed.

---

## traġiċità (n-traġiċità)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: tragic nature, tragicness
    **MT**: Kwalità u sensazzjoni ta' niket u traġedja.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-traġiċità tas-sitwazzjoni touched lil kulħadd.
    EN: The tragicness of the situation touched everyone.

---

## traħħam (v-traħħam)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: r-ħ-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to be covered with marble
    **MT**: Ġie miżbugħ jew miksi b'marbol jew blat.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-ħajt traħħam f'kulur abjad u griż fin.
    EN: The wall was covered with fine white and grey marble.

---

## traħħas (v-traħħas)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: r-ħ-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to drop in price, become cheaper
    **MT**: Mar u tnaqqas il-prezz tiegħu f'bejgħ.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-frott traħħas fis-suq filgħaxija.
    EN: The fruit became cheaper at the market in the evening.

---

## traħħim (n-traħħim)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: r-ħ-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: marbling
    **MT**: Azzjoni u kisa bil-marbol fuq l-art jew il-ħajt.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-traħħim tas-sala sar minn artiġjan tal-ġebel.
    EN: The marbling of the hall was done by a stone craftsman.

---

## trebbaħ (v-trebbaħ)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: r-b-ħ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to be enriched, win a prize
    **MT**: Ġie magħżul bħala rebbieħ u rċieva vantaġġ.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Trebbaħ il-premju prinċipali fit-lotterija tar-raħal.
    EN: He won the main prize in the village lottery.

---

## trebbiħ (n-trebbiħ)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: r-b-ħ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: making win, enriching
    **MT**: Azzjoni li trejbaħ u tfewwaq lil xi ħadd.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-trebbiħ tat-tim kien riżultat ta' sforz sħiħ.
    EN: Making the team win was the result of full effort.

## tregħib (v-tregħib)
- **POS**: verb
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: r-għ-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to make greedy
    **MT**: Azzjoni li tregħeb u tiġbed persuna bil-flus.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għamel tregħib fuq in-nies bl-akbar wegħdiet.
    EN: He enticed people with the greatest promises.

---

## trejjaq (v-trejjaq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: r-j-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to eat breakfast, nourish oneself
    **MT**: Kiel u ta nutrizzjoni lill-korp fuq stonku vojt.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Trejjaq sew b'ħobż u te qabel ma mar jaħdem.
    EN: He ate a good breakfast with bread and tea before work.

---

## trejjiq (n-trejjiq)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: r-j-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: nourishing, breakfasting
    **MT**: L-att li tiekol u ssostni il-korp filgħodu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-trejjiq fl-għodwa huwa importanti ħafna għas-saħħa.

## trejn (n-trejn)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: train (rail vehicle)
    **MT**: Vettura bir-roti fuq linji tal-ħadid li iġorr nies u oġġetti.
- **Usage Examples**:

    EN: They took the train to travel between cities in Europe.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: small street, alley (diminutive)
    **MT**: Triq żgħira u dejqa fir-raħal qadim.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Imxew fit-trejqa fost il-ħwienet żgħar.
    EN: They walked in the small alley among small shops.

- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: r-k-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to cower, huddle
    **MT**: Stahba u rduppa lilu nnifsu f'rokna bir-biża'.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Trekken fir-rokna meta sama' it-twerqa.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: train (rail vehicle)

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Rabu it-tren biex jivvjaġġaw lejn Ruma.
    EN: They took the train to travel to Rome.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: r-ġ-j

- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: r-ġ-għ

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: r-ġ-għ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: returning, restoring
    **MT**: L-att li tirritorna oġġett lura lil sidu.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine

  - **EN**: tribute, honour
    **MT**: Rigal jew unur mogħti biex turi rispett lil persuna.
- **Usage Examples**:

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tribù kienet tgħix fil-foresta u tikkaċċja.

- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: r-għ-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to become greedy
    **MT**: Mela b'rgħba kbira għall-flus u l-proprjetà.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Triegħeb fuq id-domanda tal-art u tilef sħabhu.

- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: r-għ-d

  - **EN**: to tremble, shake

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Triegħed bir-riħ kiesaħ matul il-lejl u fit-triq.
    EN: He trembled with the cold wind during the night.

- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: r-ħ-j

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-ħajt triffed mir-raħħal biex ma jaqax fil-maltemp.
    EN: The wall was propped up by the builder to prevent it falling.

- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: r-f-s

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-ħaxix triffes mid-demm u mill-folla fl-istadju.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine

- **Definitions**:

- **Usage Examples**:

- **POS**: noun

  - **EN**: Tripoli (city)

- **Definitions**:
